    // A shadow primitive (0x960b20, shadow_type 2) outputs only t times the falloff times the shadow's alpha.

,v8P88P
- "Always On" aims at a constant speed without additional conditions. Intended for use with a hotkey
- "Assisted" helps with aiming based on the user's mouse movement. Movement toward the target is amplified by the aimbot
- "At Targets" rotates the set yaw relative to the angle toward the current threat
- "At Targets" sets yaw relative to the angle toward the current threat
- "Auto" calculates minimum damage based on the weapon, its fire rate, and the target's health
- "Between Shots" stops you even if the weapon is not ready to fire
- "Full" completely removes spread
- "HP+N" sets minimum damage to value equal to the target's health plus N
- "In Air" minimizes horizontal velocity by the time you reach the peak of the jump. Designed for "jump-scouting"
- "Local View" rotates the set yaw relative to the camera
- "Local View" sets yaw relative to the camera
- "On Attack" aims at a constant speed while holding down the attack key (LMB). The user's mouse movement is ignored
- "On Land" prevents you from bunny-hopping if the aimbot can shoot quickly after landing
- "Seed Check" checks whether the shot will land in addition to the hit chance requirement
- "Seed" special mode that accurately predicts the current shot and guarantees a hit
- "Static" locks the set yaw as an absolute rotation
- "Sync" uses the minimum damage value from the triggerbot
- 0%: No subticks (standard strafer)
- 0%: Turn instantly (no acceleration attempt)
- 100%: Gain speed while turning (slower rotations)
- 100%: Maximum number of subticks
- Bomb damage is shown when 5 seconds or less remain until the explosion. A contributing bomb sets the pulse speed.
- HE includes thrown HE grenades and your held HE when Grenade Trajectory or Automatic Grenade Release provides a prediction.
- Inherit uses the health bar color at the start of the damage segment; Custom uses two chosen pulse colors.
- Molotovs includes thrown incendiaries; Infernos includes live fire. Contact with fire shows one second of base damage from the server settings per source, without ramp-up or duration calculations.
- With Safe Mode: follows your mouse direction naturally
- Without Safe Mode: provides perfect automated strafing (TAS-like)

; if (keep.wlLobbyHooked6) return; keep.wlLobbyHooked6 = true; var findPopup = function () {  var mark = root.FindChildTraverse('AcceptMatchCountdown') || root.FindChildTraverse('AcceptMatchSlots');  if (!mark || !mark.IsValid()) return null;  var panel = mark; var last = mark;  while (panel && panel.GetParent && panel.GetParent()) {   var type = ''; try { type = panel.paneltype || ''; } catch (e) {}   if (type.indexOf('Popup') >= 0) return panel;   last = panel; panel = panel.GetParent();   if (panel === root) break;  }  return last; }; var frameOf = function (popup) {  var mark = root.FindChildTraverse('AcceptMatchCountdown') || root.FindChildTraverse('AcceptMatchSlots');  var best = null; var limit = popup.actuallayoutwidth * 0.9;  for (var p = mark; p && p !== popup && p.GetParent; p = p.GetParent()) { if (p.actuallayoutwidth > 0 && p.actuallayoutwidth < limit) best = p; }  return best; }; var place = function (popup, button) {  var frame = frameOf(popup); if (!frame || !frame.GetPositionWithinWindow || !popup.GetPositionWithinWindow) return;  var scale = popup.actualuiscale_x || 1; var a = frame.GetPositionWithinWindow(); var b = popup.GetPositionWithinWindow();  var x = (a.x - b.x + frame.actuallayoutwidth) / scale - 44; var y = (a.y - b.y) / scale + 14;  button.style.horizontalAlign = 'left'; button.style.verticalAlign = 'top'; button.style.margin = '0px';  button.style.position = Math.round(x) + 'px ' + Math.round(y) + 'px 0px'; }; var cancelMatch = function (popup) {  try { UiToolkitAPI.HideTextTooltip(); } catch (e) {}  try { LobbyAPI.StopMatchmaking(); } catch (e) {}  try { $.DispatchEvent('CloseAcceptPopup'); } catch (e) {}  try { if (popup && popup.IsValid()) $.DispatchEvent('UIPopupButtonClicked', popup, ''); } catch (e) {} }; var addCross = function (tries) {  if (!keep.wlCancelMatch) return;  var popup = findPopup();  if (!popup) {   if (tries < 40) { $.Schedule(0.25, function () { addCross(tries + 1); }); return; }   $.Msg('[neverwin] cancel match | accept popup not found under ' + root.id + ' (' + root.paneltype + ')');   return;  }  var button = popup.FindChildTraverse('wlCancelMatch');  if (!button) {   button = $.CreatePanel('Button', popup, 'wlCancelMatch');   button.style.width = '30px'; button.style.height = '30px';   button.style.horizontalAlign = 'right'; button.style.verticalAlign = 'top'; button.style.margin = '14px';   button.style.zIndex = '1000';   var icon = $.CreatePanel('Image', button, 'wlCancelMatchIcon');   icon.SetImage('file://{images}/icons/ui/cancel.svg');   icon.style.width = '100%'; icon.style.height = '100%'; icon.style.washColor = '#ffffff96';   button.SetPanelEvent('onmouseover', function () { icon.style.washColor = '#ffffff'; try { UiToolkitAPI.ShowTextTooltipOnPanel(button, 'cancel match'); } catch (e) {} });   button.SetPanelEvent('onmouseout', function () { icon.style.washColor = '#ffffff96'; try { UiToolkitAPI.HideTextTooltip(); } catch (e) {} });   button.SetPanelEvent('onactivate', function () { cancelMatch(popup); });  }  place(popup, button);  $.Schedule(0.5, function () { var now = findPopup(); if (now) addCross(0); }); }; $.RegisterForUnhandledEvent('PanoramaComponent_Lobby_ReadyUpForMatch', function (shouldShow) {  if (keep.wlAutoAccept) { try { $.DispatchEvent('MatchAssistedAccept'); } catch (e) {} }  if (!shouldShow) return;  if (keep.wlCancelMatch) $.Schedule(0.2, function () { addCross(0); }); });} catch (e) { $.Msg('[neverwin] lobby script | ' + e); } })();

vector_mt.__add = function(a, b) a, b = as_vector(a), as_vector(b) return vector(a.x + b.x, a.y + b.y, a.z + b.z) end
vector_mt.__div = function(a, b) a, b = as_vector(a), as_vector(b) return vector(a.x / b.x, a.y / b.y, a.z / b.z) end
vector_mt.__eq = function(a, b) return a.x == b.x and a.y == b.y and a.z == b.z end
vector_mt.__index = vector_mt
vector_mt.__mul = function(a, b) a, b = as_vector(a), as_vector(b) return vector(a.x * b.x, a.y * b.y, a.z * b.z) end
vector_mt.__sub = function(a, b) a, b = as_vector(a), as_vector(b) return vector(a.x - b.x, a.y - b.y, a.z - b.z) end
vector_mt.__tostring = function(a) return format('vector(%.3f, %.3f, %.3f)', a.x, a.y, a.z) end
vector_mt.__unm = function(a) return vector(-a.x, -a.y, -a.z) end
